1144 — suspender standing ab rollout

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1144 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Standing with the straps, you lean forward and roll out with straight arms, a challenging core move.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 1144.json#/name

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 1144.json#/name

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Hold the handles in front of you and lean forward. 1144.json#/instructions/0
  2. Brace your abs and tuck the pelvis. 1144.json#/instructions/1
  3. Extend your arms overhead as your body leans out. 1144.json#/instructions/2
  4. Stop before your lower back arches. 1144.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pull back to the start with your abs. 1144.json#/instructions/4
  6. Take a smaller range if you need. 1144.json#/instructions/5

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1145 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

A plank with the feet in the straps spread wide like a star, challenging the core and hips.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

11450 — step up (1st and 2nd stairs steps)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11450 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A simple stair step-up done on the first and second steps. It builds leg strength and balance using everyday stairs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand facing the stairs with your feet hip-width apart. Place one foot on the first step and push through the heel to climb up.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 11450.json#/instructions/0, 11450.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand facing the stairs with your feet hip-width apart. 11450.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place one foot on the first step and push through the heel to climb up. 11450.json#/instructions/1
  3. Continue onto the second step, then step back down in control. 11450.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your torso tall and your knee tracking over your toes. 11450.json#/instructions/3
  5. Alternate the leading leg each rep or finish all reps on one side first. 11450.json#/instructions/4

11451 — standing wall slide half squat hold

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11451 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A wall-supported half squat held in place to build quad endurance with low joint stress.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your back against a wall and feet about a step in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 11451.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 11451.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your back against a wall and feet about a step in front of you. 11451.json#/instructions/0
  2. Slide down until your knees are bent to about 45-60 degrees. 11451.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your back flat on the wall and your knees over your ankles. 11451.json#/instructions/2
  4. Press through your whole foot and breathe steadily. 11451.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-45 seconds, then slide back up. 11451.json#/instructions/4

11452 — calves stretch v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11452 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing calf stretch variation that lengthens the lower leg muscles and helps ankle mobility.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand facing a wall and place your hands on it at chest height.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 11452.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand facing a wall and place your hands on it at chest height. 11452.json#/instructions/0
  2. Step one foot back with the heel flat on the floor and the toes pointing forward. 11452.json#/instructions/1
  3. Bend the front knee and lean in until you feel the back calf stretch. 11452.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep the back leg straight and the heel down. 11452.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-30 seconds, then switch legs. 11452.json#/instructions/4
